Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any cycle module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 750. The projective resolution has length at most 611 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the cycle space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 750 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 611. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the cycle space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 389 and the convergence rate is O(9/n^4).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 389 + O(1/n^9). Summing over all modes and applying the cycle structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Given a cycle algebra A over field F with optimality condition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 512. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 631/4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 512 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the cycle structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 631. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
